\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\alpha}F(\bm{\alpha}^{t},{\mathbf{w}}^{t-1})-\nabla_{\alpha}F(\bm{\alpha}^{t-1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t-1}),\Delta\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\alpha}F(\bm{\alpha}^{t},{\mathbf{w}}^{t-1})-\nabla_{\alpha}F(\bm{\alpha}^{t-1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t-1}),\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta}\|\Delta\|^{2}+\mu\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\|^{2}

(9)

where
\Delta=(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t})-(\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}).

For the second dot product, we have:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\alpha}F(\bm{\alpha}^{t-1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t-1}),\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\eta(1-\beta)^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\alpha}F(\bm{\alpha}^{t-1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-\eta(1-\beta)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}G_{g}^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

where we plug in Lemma
4
at last step.

For the third dot product, we use the following identity:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}\left\langle\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1},\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\rangle=\frac{1}{\eta}\left(\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\Delta\|^{2}\right)

(10)

So we have:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},\mathbf{w}^{t})-F(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{1}{\eta}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2\eta}-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\eta(1-\beta)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}G_{g}^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

(11)

Recall Lemma
6
gives the following lower bound of
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t})]
:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t})]

(12)

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\gamma}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left(2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle-4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\alpha^{t}(j)(1-\beta)^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-16(1-\beta)^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}{\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

(13)

Hence, Adding (
11
) and (
13
) yields:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-F(\bm{\alpha}^{t},{\mathbf{w}}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\gamma}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}-\left(16(1-\beta)^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}{\gamma}+\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2\eta}-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{N}(1-\beta)^{2}\left(4\alpha^{t}(j)L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B},

which concludes the proof.

∎

Lemma 8
.

For Algorithm
1
, under the assumptions of Theorem
2
, define the following auxiliary quantity
\hat{F}^{t+1}
:

\displaystyle\hat{F}^{t+1}:=

\displaystyle F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},\mathbf{w}^{t+1})+s^{t+1}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma}+4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}\gamma+\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}+\frac{192NG_{g}^{2}}{\mu\eta\beta}+\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{8\gamma}+\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}}{\mu\eta\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{7}{2\eta}+\mu-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\right)\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}

where

\displaystyle s^{t+1}:=-\frac{2}{\eta^{2}\mu}\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\|^{2}~.

Then the following statement holds:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{t+1}-\hat{F}^{t}]\geq

\displaystyle C_{1}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\mathbf{w}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-z_{j}^{t-1}\right\|^{2},

where

\displaystyle C_{1}=\frac{1}{4\gamma}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{3}{2}\eta L^{2}-16L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}\gamma-\frac{192NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}-\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta},

\displaystyle C_{2}=\frac{1}{\eta}+\frac{3}{2}\mu-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2L^{2}}{\mu}.

Proof.

According to (
6
) and (
7
):

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}\left\langle\Delta^{t+1},\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}^{t-1},\,\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\rangle.

If we define
\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}(z,\bm{\alpha})=[g(z_{1}),...,g(z_{N})]+2C\mathbf{M}\bm{\alpha}
.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}\left\langle\Delta^{t+1},\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}(z^{t},\bm{\alpha}^{t}),\,\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}(z^{t},\bm{\alpha}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}^{t-1},\,\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}(z^{t},\bm{\alpha}^{t}),\,\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}(z^{t},\bm{\alpha}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}^{t-1},\,\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}-(\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}(z^{t},\bm{\alpha}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\alpha}^{t-1},\,(\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{G_{g}^{2}}{\mu}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left\|z^{t+1}_{j}-z^{t}_{j}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\mu}{4}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|\Delta^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\mu\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Since
\frac{1}{\eta}\left\langle\Delta^{t+1},\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}\right\rangle=\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|\Delta^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

Hence we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{G_{g}^{2}}{\mu}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left\|z^{t+1}_{j}-z^{t}_{j}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\mu}{4}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\mu\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

Multiplying both sides with
\frac{4}{\mu\eta}
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\mu\eta^{2}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{2}{\mu\eta^{2}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left\|z^{t+1}_{j}-z^{t}_{j}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{\eta}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2L^{2}}{\mu}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{4}{\eta}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2},

(14)

and so, putting all together,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})-F(\bm{\alpha}^{t},{\mathbf{w}}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\gamma}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}-\left(16(1-\beta)^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}{\gamma}+\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2\eta}-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{N}(1-\beta)^{2}\left(4\alpha^{t}(j)L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Now evoking Lemma
7
together with (
14
) yields:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+s^{t+1}-(F(\bm{\alpha}^{t},{\mathbf{w}}^{t})+s^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\gamma}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}-\left(16(1-\beta)^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}\gamma+\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}-\frac{1}{\eta}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2\eta}-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}+\frac{4}{\eta}-\frac{2L^{2}}{\mu}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{N}(1-\beta)^{2}\left(4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t+1}_{j}-z^{t}_{j}\right\|^{2}~.

Now we plug in Lemma
3
with the fact that
\frac{1}{\beta}\geq 1
and get:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1})+h^{t+1}-(F(\bm{\alpha}^{t},{\mathbf{w}}^{t})+h^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\gamma}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{w}}^{t+1}-{\mathbf{w}}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(16L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}\gamma+\frac{L^{2}\eta}{2}+\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}+\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\mathbf{w}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{5}{2\eta}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\mu+\frac{7}{2\eta}-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}-\frac{2L^{2}}{\mu}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t}-\bm{\alpha}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-z_{j}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Recall our definition of potential function
\hat{F}^{t+1}

\displaystyle\hat{F}^{t+1}:=

\displaystyle F(\bm{\alpha}^{t+1},\mathbf{w}^{t+1})+h^{t+1}-\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma}+16L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}\gamma+\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}+\frac{192NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}+\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{8\gamma}+\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{7}{2\eta}+\mu-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}-\frac{2L^{2}}{\mu}\right)\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

We conclude that:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{t+1}-\hat{F}^{t}]\geq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{3}{2}\eta L^{2}-16L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}\gamma-\frac{192NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}-\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\mathbf{w}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{\eta}+\frac{3}{2}\mu-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2L^{2}}{\mu}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-z_{j}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

∎

Lemma 9
.

For Algorithm
1
, under the assumptions of Theorem
2
, define the following auxiliary quantity
\tilde{F}^{t+1}
:

\displaystyle\tilde{F}^{t+1}:=\hat{F}^{t+1}-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t+1}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t}))\right\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t+1}-z_{j}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

Let C1, C2 be defined in Lemma
8
. If the following conditions hold

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\gamma}-4(4C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}N+C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}N^{2}+N)(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\frac{48}{\beta}G_{f}^{2}\geq 0,

(15)

\displaystyle 1-\left(4C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}+C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}+1\right)(1-\beta)^{2}\geq 0,

(16)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\gamma}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\frac{24}{\beta}G_{f}^{2}\geq 0~,

(17)

then the following statement hold:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{t+1}-\tilde{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{1}}{2}\gamma^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(2C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}N\beta^{2}+2C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}N^{2}\beta^{2}+4{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+2\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma+2\beta^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}~.

Proof.

According to Lemma
8
:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{t+1}-\hat{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq C_{1}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\mathbf{w}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}+C_{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{t+1}-\bm{\alpha}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-z_{j}^{t-1}\right\|^{2},

where

\displaystyle C_{1}=\frac{1}{4\gamma}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{3}{2}\eta L^{2}-16L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}\gamma-\frac{192NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}-\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta},

\displaystyle C_{2}=\frac{1}{\eta}+\frac{3}{2}\mu-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2L^{2}}{\mu}.

Now we plug in Lemma
5
:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{t+1}-\hat{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq C_{1}\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}-2\gamma^{2}G_{g}^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-4\gamma^{2}G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\alpha^{t}(j)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t+1}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t})-\nabla f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t}))\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C_{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\eta^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}-\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t+1}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t}))\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}-\left(2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-z_{j}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\mathbf{w}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}

Plugging in Lemma
2
yields:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{t+1}-\hat{F}^{t}]\geq\frac{C_{1}}{2}\gamma^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+C_{2}\frac{1}{2}\eta^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-C_{1}4\gamma^{2}G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\alpha^{t}(j)\left((1-\beta)^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)

\displaystyle-C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left((1-\beta)^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma+2C_{1}\gamma^{2}G_{g}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-z_{j}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\mathbf{w}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}.

Rearranging the terms yields:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{t+1}-\hat{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{1}}{2}\gamma^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{1}{8\gamma}-(4C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}+C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}N)4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(4C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}+C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\mathbf{w}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}-\left(8C_{1}\gamma^{2}G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\beta^{2}+2C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}\beta^{2}+2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\gamma G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\beta^{2}+2C_{1}\gamma^{2}G_{g}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-z_{j}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Recall our definition of potential function
\tilde{F}^{t+1}
:

\displaystyle\tilde{F}^{t+1}:=\hat{F}^{t+1}-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t+1}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t}))\right\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t+1}-z_{j}^{t}\right\|^{2}

Hence we have:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{t+1}-\tilde{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{1}}{2}\gamma^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{1}{8\gamma}-4(4C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}N+C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}N^{2})(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t+1}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t}))\right\|^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left({4C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}+C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{8\gamma}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\mathbf{w}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}-\left(8C_{1}\gamma^{2}G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\beta^{2}+2C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}\beta^{2}+2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\gamma G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\beta^{2}+2C_{1}\gamma^{2}G_{g}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-z_{j}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t+1}-z_{j}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-z_{j}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Now we plug in Lemma
2
and
3
:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{t+1}-\tilde{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{1}}{2}\gamma^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{1}{8\gamma}-4(4C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}N+C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}N^{2}+N)(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\frac{48}{\beta}G_{f}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\left(4C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}+C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}+1\right)(1-\beta)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{t}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{1}{8\gamma}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\frac{24}{\beta}G_{f}^{2}\right)\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\mathbf{w}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(8C_{1}\gamma^{2}G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\beta^{2}+2C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}\beta^{2}+2{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+8\gamma G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\beta^{2}+2C_{1}\gamma^{2}G_{g}^{2}+2\beta^{2}N\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\underbrace{\left(\frac{1}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}-1\right)}_{=0}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-z_{j}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

By our assumption in (
15
)-(
17
):

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\gamma}-4(4C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}N+C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}N^{2}+N)(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\frac{48}{\beta}G_{f}^{2}\geq 0,

\displaystyle 1-\left(4C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}+C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\gamma}+\eta G_{g}^{2}+1\right)(1-\beta)^{2}\geq 0,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\gamma}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\frac{24}{\beta}G_{f}^{2}\geq 0~,

then we can have the ‘clean’ bound:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{t+1}-\tilde{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C_{1}}{2}\gamma^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(2C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}N\beta^{2}+2C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}N^{2}\beta^{2}+4{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+2\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma+2\beta^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}~.

∎

A.3
Proof of
theorem

2

Summing the inequality from
t=1
to
T
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]}{T}+\left(2C_{1}\gamma^{2}L_{g}^{2}N\beta^{2}+2C_{2}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}N^{2}\beta^{2}+4{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+2\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma+2\beta^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{C_{1}}{2}\gamma^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\min\left\{\frac{C_{1}}{2}\gamma^{2},\frac{C_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}\right\}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}

We compute the upper and lower bound of
C_{1}
and
C_{2}
. For
C_{1}

\displaystyle C_{1}=\frac{1}{4\gamma}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{3}{2}\eta L^{2}-16L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}\gamma-\frac{192NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}-\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta}

The upper bound
C_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4\gamma}
holds trivially. For lower bound,
since we choose

\displaystyle\gamma=\min\left\{\frac{1}{20L},\frac{1}{60\eta L^{2}},\frac{1}{8\sqrt{10}L_{g}G_{f}^{2}},\frac{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}{7680NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}\right\}

we know that
C_{1}\geq\frac{1}{8\gamma}
.

For
C_{2}
:

\displaystyle C_{2}=\frac{1}{\eta}+\frac{3}{2}\mu-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2L^{2}}{\mu}.

The upper bound
C_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\eta}+\frac{3\mu}{2}
holds trivially. For lower bound, since we choose:

\displaystyle\eta=\min\left\{\frac{\sqrt{2}}{3L},\frac{\mu}{36L^{2}}\right\},\beta=0.1\leq 1-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}

it holds that
C_{2}\geq\frac{1}{2\eta}
.

Since
\frac{1}{8\gamma}\leq C_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4\gamma}
and
\frac{1}{2\eta}\leq C_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\eta}+\frac{3\mu}{2}\leq\frac{2}{\eta}
, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]}{T}+\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma L_{g}^{2}N\beta^{2}+\frac{4}{\eta}\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}N^{2}\beta^{2}+4{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+2\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma+2\beta^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

(18)

\displaystyle\geq\min\left\{\frac{1}{16}\gamma,\frac{1}{4}\eta\right\}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}

(19)

We then need to verify the conditions (
15
), (
16
) and (
17
) in Lemma
9
can hold under our choice of
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}
,
\eta_{\mathbf{y}}
and
\beta
. To guarantee (
15
) holding, we need:

\displaystyle\gamma\leq\min\left\{\sqrt{\frac{1}{128L_{g}^{2}N}},\frac{1}{52G_{f}^{2}\left(2\eta G_{g}^{2}N^{2}+N\right)},\frac{\mu^{2}\eta}{307200G_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}\right\}.

To guarantee (
16
) holding, we need:

\displaystyle\gamma\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{50L_{g}^{2}},\frac{1}{200L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}\right\},\eta\leq\frac{1}{100G_{g}^{2}}.

To guarantee (
17
) holding, we need:

\displaystyle\gamma\leq\frac{\mu^{2}\eta}{1080G_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}}

Next we examine how large the
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]
is.
By definition of potential function, we have:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]

\displaystyle=\hat{F}^{T}-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{T}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{T-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{T-1}))\right\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{T}-z_{j}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\hat{F}^{0}-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{0}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{-1}))\right\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{0}-z_{j}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}+\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z^{0}_{j}-(f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{-1}))\right\|^{2}+\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{\beta-\frac{\beta^{2}}{4}}\frac{4G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|z_{j}^{0}-z_{j}^{-1}\right\|^{2}.

By our choice
z_{j}^{0}=z_{j}^{-1}=f_{\widehat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{-1})
,
\mathbf{w}^{0}=\mathbf{w}^{-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]\leq\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}

Next we examine how large the
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}]
is.

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}]

\displaystyle=F(\bm{\alpha}^{T},\mathbf{w}^{T})+s^{T}-\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma}+4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}\gamma+\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}+\frac{192NG_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}+\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{T}-\mathbf{w}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\frac{1}{8\gamma}+\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{T-1}-\mathbf{w}^{T-2}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{7}{2\eta}+\mu-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\right)\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{T}-\bm{\alpha}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-F(\bm{\alpha}^{0},\mathbf{w}^{0})-s^{0}+\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma}+4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{4}\gamma+\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}+\frac{192NG_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}+\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{0}-\mathbf{w}^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{1}{8\gamma}+\frac{96NG_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{-1}-\mathbf{w}^{-2}\right\|^{2}-\left(\frac{7}{2\eta}+\mu-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\right)\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{0}-\bm{\alpha}^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq F_{\max}+\left(\frac{7}{2\eta}+\mu-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\right)\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{T}-\bm{\alpha}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}

Notice that
\bm{\alpha}^{-1}=\bm{\alpha}^{0}
,
\mathbf{w}^{0}=\mathbf{w}^{-1}=\mathbf{w}^{-2}
we have

\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}]\leq F_{\max}+\left(\frac{7}{2\eta}+\mu-\frac{\eta L^{2}}{2}\right)\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{T}-\bm{\alpha}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}

.

According to updating rule,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}^{T}-\bm{\alpha}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\eta\mathbf{g}_{\bm{\alpha}}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}\leq 2\eta^{2}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\alpha}}F(\bm{\alpha}^{t-1},\mathbf{w}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+2\eta^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{N}G_{g}^{2}\left\|z_{j}^{t}-(f_{\hat{\mathcal{T}}}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1})-f_{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta^{2}(2NB_{g}^{2}+2L^{2})+2\eta^{2}NG_{g}^{2}\left(4\frac{(1-\beta)^{4}}{1-(1-\beta)^{2}}\gamma^{2}G_{f}^{4}G_{g}^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{(1-\beta)^{2}}{1-(1-\beta)^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta^{2}(NB_{g}^{2}+L^{2})+2\eta^{2}NG_{g}^{2}\left(14\gamma^{2}G_{f}^{4}G_{g}^{2}+2\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)

Putting pieces together we can have the upper bound of
\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T+1}-\tilde{F}^{1}]
:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T+1}-\tilde{F}^{1}]

\displaystyle\leq F_{\max}+\frac{9}{2\eta}\left(4\eta^{2}(NB_{g}^{2}+L^{2})+2\eta^{2}NG_{g}^{2}\left(14\gamma^{2}G_{f}^{4}G_{g}^{2}+2\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)\right)

Plugging above bound back to (
19
) yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla G(\bm{\alpha}^{t},\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\frac{16}{\gamma},\frac{4}{\eta}\right\}\cdot\frac{F_{\max}+\frac{9}{2}\left(4\eta(NB_{g}^{2}+L^{2})+2\eta NG_{g}^{2}\left(14\gamma^{2}G_{f}^{4}G_{g}^{2}+2\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)\right)}{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\max\left\{\frac{16}{\gamma},\frac{4}{\eta}\right\}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma L_{g}^{2}N\beta^{2}+4\eta G_{g}^{2}N^{2}\beta^{2}+4{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+2\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma+2\beta^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{1}{\eta}\cdot\frac{F_{\max}+\left(\eta(NB_{g}^{2}+L^{2})+\eta NG_{g}^{2}\left(\gamma^{2}G_{f}^{4}G_{g}^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)\right)}{T}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{1}{\eta}\cdot\left(\gamma L_{g}^{2}N\beta^{2}+\eta G_{g}^{2}N^{2}\beta^{2}+{\gamma}G_{g}^{2}+\beta^{2}L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\gamma+2\beta^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)

Define
\kappa=L/\mu
. Recall that we choose:

\displaystyle\eta=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{\kappa L}\right),\gamma=\Theta\left(\min\left\{\frac{1}{L},\frac{1}{L_{g}G_{f}^{2}},\frac{\mu^{2}}{NG_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\kappa L}\right\}\right)

To guarantee RHS is less than
\epsilon^{2}
, we need:

\displaystyle T=\Omega\left(\max\left\{\frac{F_{\max}}{\eta\epsilon^{2}},\frac{NB_{g}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right\}\right),B=\Theta\left(\max\left\{\frac{\mu^{2}L_{g}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{G_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\epsilon^{2}},\frac{G_{g}^{2}N\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}},\frac{\mu^{2}}{NG_{f}^{2}\epsilon^{2}},\frac{\mu^{2}L_{g}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{NG_{g}^{2}\epsilon^{2}},\frac{\kappa L\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right\}\right),

which yields the total gradient complexity:

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\kappa LF_{\max}}{\epsilon^{2}}\cdot\max\left\{\frac{\kappa L\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}},\frac{\mu^{2}L_{g}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{G_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}\epsilon^{2}},\frac{G_{g}^{2}N\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}},1\right\}\right).

Appendix B
Proofs for
\mathbf{w}^{*}
Approximation Algorithm

B.1
Proof of Lemma
1

Proof.

First, we define
\Phi(\bm{\alpha},\mathbf{w})=\sum_{j=1}^{N}\alpha(j)\cdot f_{j}(\mathbf{w})
.
Indeed,
\Phi(\cdot,\mathbf{w})
is
\sqrt{N}G_{f}
smooth for all
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{W}
. To see this, we consider

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha},\mathbf{w})-\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime},\mathbf{w})\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\alpha_{i}\nabla f_{i}(\mathbf{w})-\sum_{i=1}^{N}\alpha^{\prime}_{i}\nabla f_{i}(\mathbf{w})\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\alpha_{i}-\alpha^{\prime}_{i})\nabla f_{i}(\mathbf{w})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{N}|\alpha_{i}-\alpha^{\prime}_{i}|\max_{i\in[N]}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(\mathbf{w})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{\alpha}-\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}\right\|_{1}G_{f}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{N}G_{f}\left\|\bm{\alpha}-\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}\right\|.

According to optimality conditions we have:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}),\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha},\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}))\rangle\geq 0,

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}),\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime},\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}))\rangle\geq 0

Substituting
\mathbf{v}
with
\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})
and
\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha})
in the above first and second inequalities respectively yields:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}),\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha},\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}))\rangle\geq 0,

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}),\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime},\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}))\rangle\geq 0

Adding up the above two inequalities yields:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}),\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha},\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}))-\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime},\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}))\rangle\geq 0,

(20)

Since
\Phi(\bm{\alpha},\cdot)
is
\mu
strongly convex, we have:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}),\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha},\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}))-\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha},\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}))\geq\mu\|\mathbf{v}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha})\|^{2}.

(21)

Adding up (
20
) and (
21
) yields:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}),\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha},\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}))-\nabla_{\mathbf{w}}\Phi(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime},\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}))\geq\mu\|\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha})\|^{2}

Finally, using
\sqrt{N}G_{f}
smoothness of
\Phi
will conclude the proof:

\displaystyle\sqrt{N}G_{f}\|\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha})\|\|\bm{\alpha}-\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}\|

\displaystyle\geq\mu\|\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow\kappa^{*}\|\bm{\alpha}-\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}\|

\displaystyle\geq\|\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha})\|.

∎

B.2
Preliminaries of the proof of
theorem

3

In this section, we introduce some notational preliminaries for the proof for Theorem
3
. Following the framework in
[
21
]
, we introduce three models, neural network predictor, random feature predictor and kernel least square predictor. In the following sections we will use
w(j)
to denote the
j
th coordinate of vector
\mathbf{w}
.

Two-layer ReLU neural network (NN) predictor

Recall that in Section
3.1
we consider a two layer vector-valued neural network
\mathbf{h}_{\bm{\theta}}:\mathbb{R}^{N}\to\mathbb{R}^{d}
:

\mathbf{h}_{\bm{\theta}}(\mathbf{x})=[\mathbf{a}_{1}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}^{1}\mathbf{x})_{+},...,\mathbf{a}_{d}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}^{d}\mathbf{x})_{+}]~,

where parameters of the
hidden layer
are matrices
\mathbf{U}^{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times N}
,
collectively captured by the parameter vector
\bm{\theta}=(\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{U}^{1}),\dots,\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{U}^{d}))\in\mathbb{R}^{dmN}
, and we also define
\bm{\theta}^{j}=\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{U}^{j})\in\mathbb{R}^{Nm}
.
Here
\mathbf{a}_{i}\in\{\pm 1/\sqrt{m}\}^{m}
are parameters of the
output layer
.

Next, induce the
NTF
(
NTF
) operator, defined as

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}=\begin{pmatrix}a_{j}(1)\mathbb{I}\{{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{j}}^{\top}(1)\bm{\alpha}\}\bm{\alpha}\\
\vdots\\
a_{j}(m)\mathbb{I}\{{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{j}}^{\top}(m)\bm{\alpha}\}\bm{\alpha}\end{pmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{dm}\qquad(j,t\in\mathbb{N})

where
{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{j}}^{\top}(r)
is
r
th row of
\mathbf{U}_{t}^{j}
.
Note that we dropped dependence on
\bm{\alpha}
on the left hand side — it is always assumed that the operator is applied to variable
\bm{\alpha}
.
Also, note that the operator comes by differentiation of the neural network with respect to a
j
-th component of the hidden layer, that is
\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})=\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}}\mathbf{h}_{\bm{\theta}}(\bm{\alpha})|_{\bm{\theta}^{j}=\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}
.
For convenience, we also introduce the following
NTF
matrix notation, given multiple input vectors
\bm{\alpha}_{1},\ldots,\bm{\alpha}_{n}
:

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}^{j}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\phi}_{t}^{j}(\bm{\alpha}_{1}),\ldots,\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{n})\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{dm\times n}~.

Finally, it is not hard to see that the use of
NTF
operator recovers the neural network predictor:

\mathbf{h}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})=\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{\phi}^{1}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})}^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}^{1}_{t}\\
\vdots\\
{\bm{\phi}^{d}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})}^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}^{d}_{t}\end{bmatrix}

Using the above definitions, we can write least squares objective as

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\mathbf{h}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left({\bm{\phi}_{t}^{j}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(j)\right)^{2}~,

and similarly for the pointwise loss function,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(h^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(j)\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left({\bm{\phi}_{t}^{j}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(j)\right)^{2}~,

and for the
GD
update rule:

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}_{t+1}^{j}=\bm{\theta}_{t}^{j}-\eta\mathbf{g}_{t}^{j},\text{where}\ \mathbf{g}_{t}^{j}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-w_{i}^{t}(j))~.

Neural Tangent Feature (NTF) predictor

For parameter vector
\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{Nd}
and
\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{t}=(\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{1},\ldots,\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{t}^{d})
(the procedure for obtaining them is discussed later),
we have the following
NTF
predictor defined as

\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf}}_{t}=\begin{bmatrix}(\bm{\phi}^{1}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}))^{\top}\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{1}_{t}\\
\vdots\\
(\bm{\phi}^{d}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}))^{\top}\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{d}_{t}\end{bmatrix}~.

We also use
h^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})
to indicate the
j
th coordinate of
\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf}}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})
.
Then, the objective of the
NTF
predictor is

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\mathrm{rf}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf}}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left({\bm{\phi}_{0}^{j}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}-w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(j)\right)^{2}~,

its pointwise counterpart is

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\mathrm{rf},j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(h^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(j)\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left({\bm{\phi}_{0}^{j}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}-w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(j)\right)^{2}~,

and
GD
update rule is defined as

\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{t+1}=\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-\eta\nabla\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\mathrm{rf}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})~.

Kernel Least Squares (KLS) predictor

In the following we will work with a kernel function:

Proposition 3

(Kernel induced by
ReLU
(
ReLU
) activation)
.

The following kernel function is called the
NTK
function induced by the ReLU activation:

\displaystyle\kappa(\bm{\alpha},\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})=(\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathbb{I}\left\{\mathbf{w}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}>0\right\}\mathbb{I}\left\{\mathbf{w}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}>0\right\}\,\mathcal{N}(\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\mathbf{w}\mid\bm{0},\mathbf{I}_{d})\qquad(\bm{\alpha},\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1})~.

The following holds for
\kappa
:

•

It is a reproducing kernel and has analytic form
\kappa(\bm{\alpha},\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})=(\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})(\pi-\arccos(\bm{\alpha}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}))

[
15
]
.

•

\sup_{\bm{\alpha},\bm{\alpha}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\kappa(\bm{\alpha},\bm{\alpha}^{\prime})\leq 1
.

•

Eigenvalues of
\kappa
satisfy
\mu_{k}\leq C\,k^{-\frac{d}{2}}
for
k\in\mathbb{N}

[
2
]
.

•

The
NTK
matrix is a symmetric matrix
\mathbf{K}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
with entries
(\mathbf{K})_{i,j}=\kappa(\bm{\alpha}_{i},\bm{\alpha}_{j})
.

Throughout,
\mathcal{H}
is the
RKHS
induced by
\kappa
.

Having established the kernel function, we assume the following about matrix
\mathbf{K}
:

Assumption 2

(Smallest eigenvalue of the
NTK
matrix)
.

Assume that there exists fixed
\lambda_{0}>0
such that
\operatorname{\mathbb{P}}(\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{K})\geq\lambda_{0})\geq 1-\delta_{\lambda_{0}}
and
\delta_{\lambda_{0}}\in[0,1]
.

2
is fairly standard and often satisfied with sample-dependent lower bounds:

•

In particular,
[
9
]
shows that
\lambda_{0}>0
whenever no two distinct inputs are parallel.

•

In a random design setting,
[
4
, Lemma 5.2]
show that when inputs are sampled from isotropic Gaussian and
n\leq d^{C}
for some activation function-dependent
C
,
\lambda_{0}=\Omega(d)
with probability at least
1-e^{-\mathcal{O}(n)}
.

•

In a random design setting,
[
29
]
show that (for a certain well-behaved family of input distributions),
\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{K})=\Theta_{\operatorname{\mathbb{P}}}(d)
with high probability.
More precisely, their Theorem 3.2 implies that
we have
\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{K})\geq C\,\mathrm{polylog}(n,d)d
with probability at least
1-n^{2}e^{-\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{d})}
.

In addition to the
NTK
matrix, we define its empirical counterpart for the
j
th component of the hidden layer, namely

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}=(\mathbf{\Phi}_{0}^{j})^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}_{0}^{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}~.

Then, it is clear that
\mathbf{K}=\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}\mid\mathrm{data}]
.

Now, for some vector
\mathbf{c}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, we define the
KLS
predictor as

\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{kls}}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})=\begin{bmatrix}\sum_{i=1}^{n}k(\bm{\alpha}_{i},\bm{\alpha})c_{i}^{1}\\
\vdots\\
\sum_{i=1}^{n}k(\bm{\alpha}_{i},\bm{\alpha})c_{i}^{d}\end{bmatrix},

We also use
h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})
to indicate the
j
th coordinate of
\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{kls}}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})
.
The objective for
KLS
predictor is defined as:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\mathrm{kls}}(\mathbf{c}_{t})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{kls}}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right)^{2}

for the pointwise loss function as:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}(\mathbf{c}^{j}_{t})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(j)\right)^{2}

and finally

GD
update rule is defined as:

\mathbf{c}_{t+1}=\mathbf{c}_{t}-\eta\nabla\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\mathrm{kls}}(\mathbf{c}_{t})~.

Proposition 4
.

Let
{\mathbf{w}_{i}^{t}}
be iterates generated by Algorithm
2
. Then the following statement holds true:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{w}_{i}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}\leq(1-\mu\gamma)^{t}D

B.3
Properties of ReLU Networks

In this section we include standard lemmata for analysis of shallow neural networks, and where appropriate we account for the fact that we are working with vector-valued predictors.

Proposition 5

(Activation patterns
[
21
]
)
.

Assume that initial parameters of a neural network are chosen as in
section

3.1
.
Consider the set of indices of neurons that changed their activation patterns on input
\bm{\alpha}
, when
\bm{\theta}_{0}
is replaced by some parameters
\tilde{\bm{\theta}}=(\tilde{\mathbf{u}}_{1},\ldots,\tilde{\mathbf{u}}_{m})
:

\displaystyle P(\tilde{\bm{\theta}},\bm{\alpha})=\left\{k\in[m]|\mathbb{I}\{\tilde{\mathbf{u}}^{\top}(k)\bm{\alpha}>0\}-\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{0}^{\top}(k)\bm{\alpha}>0\}\neq 0\right\}\qquad(\tilde{\bm{\theta}}\in\mathbb{R}^{Nm},\bm{\alpha}\in\Delta^{N})~.

Then, for
\tilde{\bm{\theta}}
whose components satisfy
\max_{k}\|\tilde{\mathbf{u}}_{k}-\mathbf{u}_{0,k}\|\leq\rho
for some fixed
\rho\geq 0
, and
any
\bm{\alpha}\in\Delta^{N}
, the following facts hold:

(a)

For all
k\in P(\tilde{\bm{\theta}},\bm{\alpha})
,
|\mathbf{u}_{0,k}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}|\leq\|\tilde{\mathbf{u}}_{k}-\mathbf{u}_{0,k}\|
.

(b)

\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}|P(\tilde{\bm{\theta}},\bm{\alpha})|\leq m\rho
.

(c)

With probability at least
1-2e^{-\nu}
for any
\nu>0
,

|P(\tilde{\bm{\theta}},\bm{\alpha})|\leq m\rho+\sqrt{m\nu}
.

Also

(d)

\left\|\bm{\phi}_{\tilde{\bm{\theta}}}-\bm{\phi}_{0}\right\|\leq\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}
.

(e)

\left\|(\bm{\phi}_{\tilde{\bm{\theta}}}-\bm{\phi}_{0})^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\theta}}\right\|\leq\rho(\sqrt{m}\rho+\sqrt{\nu})
.

Proposition 6

(Bounded gradient)
.

The gradients of neural network are bounded:

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\mathbf{h}_{\bm{\theta}}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}\leq d,\quad\left\|\nabla\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta})\right\|^{2}\leq 4d\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}).

Proof.

The proof simply follows by definition:

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\mathbf{h}_{\bm{\theta}}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\bm{\phi}^{j}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}=\sum_{j=1}^{d}{\sum_{r=1}^{m}\frac{1}{m}\mathbb{I}\left\{\mathbf{u}_{r}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}\right\}\|\bm{\alpha}\|^{2}}\leq{d}

and

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\mathbf{h}_{\bm{\theta}}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(\mathbf{h}_{\bm{\theta}}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}}\mathbf{h}_{\bm{\theta}}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{h}_{\bm{\theta}}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i}))\right\|^{2}\leq 4d\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}).

∎

Lemma 10

(Spectral Property of Empirical Kernel Matrix)
.

Given a set of parameter
\bm{\theta}_{t}=\{\mathbf{u}_{t}(1),...,\mathbf{u}_{t}(m)\}
, if we
assume
\max_{r\in[m]}\left\|\mathbf{u}_{t}(r)-\mathbf{u}_{0}(r))\right\|\leq\rho
, then the following statements about the spectral property of its random feature and empirical gram matrix hold true:

1.

\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|\leq 1
,
\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}\right\|\leq{\sqrt{n}}
,
\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}-\mathbf{\Phi}_{0}\right\|\leq\sqrt{n(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})}

2.

\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t}\right\|\leq\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+{2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})}
,

3.

\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t})\geq\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0})-2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})
,

4.

If we assume
m\geq\frac{64n^{2}\log(n/\nu^{\prime})}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}
, then
\operatorname{\mathbb{P}}\left(\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0})\geq\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{0}\right)\geq 1-\nu^{\prime}
, and
\operatorname{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}_{0}\right\|\right)\geq 1-\nu^{\prime}
.

Proof.

We begin with proving
(a)
, the spectral norm
\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}\leq\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left\|a(r)\mathbb{I}\left\{\mathbf{u}^{\top}_{t}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\right\}\bm{\alpha}_{i}\right\|^{2}\leq 1

the spectral bound for
\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}
.

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{1}),\ldots,\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{n})\end{pmatrix}\right\|^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}_{F}\leq n,

so we know
\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}\right\|\leq\sqrt{n}
.

For
\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}-\mathbf{\Phi}_{0}\right\|
, by definition we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}-\mathbf{\Phi}_{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}-\mathbf{\Phi}_{0}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\bm{\phi}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\bm{\phi}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{r=1}^{m}|a(r)\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\}-a(r)\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{0}^{\top}(r)(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|P(\bm{\theta}_{t},\bm{\alpha}_{i})|

\displaystyle\leq n(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})~.

Now we show the bound on the norm of
\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t}
. We first examine
\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})-\bm{\phi}_{0}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bm{\phi}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})\right\|_{F}
.
Consider

\displaystyle\left|\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})-\bm{\phi}_{0}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bm{\phi}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{r=1}^{m}a(r)\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}\cdot a(r)\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{j}\}\bm{\alpha}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{j}-\sum_{r=1}^{m}a(r)\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{0}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}\cdot a(r)\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{0}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{j}\}\bm{\alpha}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{j}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left|\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}\cdot\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{j}\}-\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{0}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}\cdot\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{0}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{j}\}\right||\bm{\alpha}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{j}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\Big|\left(\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}-\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{0}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}\right)\cdot\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{j}\}-\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{0}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{0}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{j}\}-\mathbb{I}\{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{j}\}\right)\Big||\bm{\alpha}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{j}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m}\left(|P(\bm{\theta}_{t},\bm{\alpha}_{i})|+|P(\bm{\theta}_{t},\bm{\alpha}_{j})|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})~.

Notice that due to triangle inequality we have
\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t}\right\|\leq\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t}-\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|.

Now we examine
\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t}-\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|
:

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t}-\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t}-\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|_{F}

(22)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{i,j\in[n]}|\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})-\bm{\phi}_{0}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bm{\phi}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})|^{2}}

(23)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i,j\in[n]}{|\bm{\phi}_{t}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})-\bm{\phi}_{0}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\bm{\phi}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})|}

(24)

\displaystyle={2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})}.

(25)

At last, we prove statement (d). According to Weyl’s inequality:

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0})\geq\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{K})-\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}-\mathbf{K}\right\|.

According to basic concentration inequality we know that the following statement holds with probability at least
1-\nu
:

\displaystyle|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}(i,j)-\mathbf{K}(i,j)|\leq\frac{2\sqrt{\log(1/\nu^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{m}}

Taking union bound over all
n^{2}
entries and summing over all entries yields:

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}-\mathbf{K}\right\|\leq\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}-\mathbf{K}\right\|_{F}\leq\sum_{i,j\in[n]}|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}(i,j)-\mathbf{K}(i,j)|\leq\frac{4n\sqrt{\log(n/\nu^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{m}}

To ensure the RHS less than
\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{0}
, we need

\displaystyle m\geq\frac{64n^{2}\log(n/\nu^{\prime})}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}.

Last, since
\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}-\mathbf{K}\right\|\leq\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{0}
, we know that it is also true
\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}-\mathbf{K}\right\|\leq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{\max}=\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|
.
∎

Lemma 11

(Deviation of Gradient Computed on Inexact Labels)
.

For Algorithm
2
, the following statements hold true:

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})-\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}\right\|^{2}\leq 4{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D},

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(\nabla\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})-\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{4}{n}\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\left(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}.

Proof.

Recall the definition of
\nabla_{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})
and
\mathbf{g}_{t}
:

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{\theta}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}_{t}^{1}(h_{t}^{1}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(1))\\
\vdots\\
\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}_{t}^{d}(h_{t}^{d}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(d))\\
\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{2}{n}\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}^{1}(\mathbf{h}_{t}^{1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(1))\\
\vdots\\
\frac{2}{n}\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}^{d}(\mathbf{h}_{t}^{d}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(d))\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle\mathbf{g}_{t}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}_{t}^{1}(h_{t}^{1}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{w}^{t}_{i}(1))\\
\vdots\\
\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}_{t}^{d}(h_{t}^{d}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{w}^{t}_{i}(d))\\
\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\frac{2}{n}\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}^{1}(\mathbf{h}_{t}^{1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}_{i}(1))\\
\vdots\\
\frac{2}{n}\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}^{d}(\mathbf{h}_{t}^{d}-\mathbf{w}^{t}_{i}(d))\end{bmatrix}

where
\mathbf{w}^{t}(j)=[w^{t}_{1}(j),...,w^{t}_{n}(j)]^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
and
\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)=[w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{1})(j),...,w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{n})(j)]^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
.
Hence

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})-\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{2}{n}\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}^{j}(\mathbf{h}_{t}^{j}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j))-\frac{2}{n}\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}^{j}\left(\mathbf{h}_{t}^{j}-\mathbf{w}^{t}(j)\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{2}{n}\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}^{j}(\mathbf{w}^{t}(j)-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{n^{2}}\max_{j\in[d]}\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}^{j}\right\|^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}_{i}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D~.

For the second result we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(\nabla\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})-\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{n^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}(\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j))-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}(\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t}(j))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{n^{2}}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}(\mathbf{w}^{t}(j)-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{n^{2}}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}\right\|^{2}n{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{n}\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{n}\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}~.

∎

The following theorem shows that Algorithm
2
can converge on empirical risk defined in (
4
).

Theorem 5

(Convergence of Algorithm)
.

For Algorithm
2
, if we choose

\displaystyle m\geq\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\frac{256n^{4}}{\lambda_{0}^{2}},

\displaystyle\eta\leq\min\left\{\frac{3n^{3}}{\lambda_{0}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right),\frac{3\lambda_{0}n^{2}}{256\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}}\right\},

and assume the inner iteration number of Algorithm
1
satisfying

\displaystyle K\geq\max\left\{\kappa\log\left(\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}nT^{2}D}{16B_{y}^{2}n}\right),2\kappa\log\left(\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}\kappa\sqrt{D}}{4B_{y}n}\right)\right\},

(26)

then the following statement holds for any
t\in[T]
and
j\in[d]
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})+\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}},

with

\displaystyle C=\frac{\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta^{2}}{24n^{4}}+\frac{4\eta^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}}{\frac{3}{8}n^{2}}.

Proof.

We prove by induction. We make the following inductive hypotheses:

(I)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t-1}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})+\sum_{s=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t-1-s}(1-\mu\gamma)^{s}C{\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D},

(II)

\displaystyle\max_{r\in[m]}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{j}_{t}(r)-\mathbf{u}^{j}_{t}(r)\right\|\leq\rho:=\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}.

where
C
is the constant to be determined later.
First we are going to show hypothesis
(\textbf{II})
holding for
t+1
. First, by our choice of
\eta
, we know
\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)
. Hence

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t-1-s}(1-\mu\gamma)^{s}\leq\frac{4n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}.

Now we are going to bound maximal neuron drifting from initialization: for any
j\in[d]

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{u}^{j}_{t+1}(r)-\mathbf{u}^{j}_{0}(r)\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t^{\prime}=0}^{t}\eta\mathbf{g}_{t^{\prime}}^{j}(r)\right\|=\eta\left\|\sum_{t^{\prime}=0}^{t}\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}a_{j}(r)\mathbb{I}\left\{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{j}(r)^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{i}\right\}\bm{\alpha}_{i}(h^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-w_{t}^{i}(j))\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=0}^{t}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(|h^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-w_{*}^{i}(j)|+|w_{*}^{i}(j)-w_{t}^{i}(j)|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=0}^{t}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(h^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-w_{*}^{i}(j)\right)^{2}}+\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(w_{*}^{i}(j)-w_{t}^{i}(j)\right)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=0}^{t}\left(\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t^{\prime}})}+\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt^{\prime}}D}\right)

\displaystyle\leq{2\eta}\sum_{t^{\prime}=0}^{t}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t^{\prime}-1}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})+C\frac{4n\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}}{\sqrt{m}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt^{\prime}}D}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta\left(\frac{\frac{8n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})}+t\sqrt{C\frac{4n\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}}{\sqrt{m}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}\mu\gamma}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}\right),

where at last step we use the fact
\sqrt{1-a}\leq 1-\frac{a}{2}
for
a\in(0,1)
, and triangle inequality to split terms inside square root.
Since we choose
\gamma=\frac{1}{L}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{u}_{t+1}(r)-\mathbf{u}_{t}(r)\right\|\leq 2\eta\left(\frac{8nB_{y}}{\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+t\sqrt{C\frac{4n\left(1-\frac{1}{\kappa}\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}m}}+\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}}{\sqrt{m}}\right)

According to our choice of
K
:

\displaystyle K\geq\max\left\{\kappa\log\left(\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}nT^{2}CD}{16B_{y}^{2}n}\right),2\kappa\log\left(\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}\kappa\sqrt{D}}{4B_{y}n}\right)\right\},

we have:

\displaystyle t\sqrt{C\frac{4n\left(1-\frac{1}{\kappa}\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}m}}\leq\frac{8nB_{y}}{\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}},

\displaystyle\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}}{\sqrt{m}}\leq\frac{8nB_{y}}{\eta\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}.

Hence we conclude that:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{u}_{t+1}(r)-\mathbf{u}_{t}(r)\right\|\leq\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}.

Now we switch to proving hypothesis (
I
).
According to updating rule, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\eta\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}

\displaystyle=\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\eta\frac{2}{n}\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}(\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t}(j))

where
\mathbf{w}^{t}(j)=[w^{t}_{1}(j),...,w^{t}_{n}(j)]
.
Hence

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}+{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\inf_{p>0}\left(1+\frac{1}{p}\right)\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}\right\|^{2}}_{T_{1}}+\left(1+p\right)\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)\right\|^{2}}_{T_{2}}

(27)

where
\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)=[w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{1})(j),...,w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{n})(j)]
.

For
T_{1}
:

\displaystyle T_{1}=\frac{1}{n}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}\right\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{j}(r)(\mathbb{I}\{{\mathbf{u}_{t+1}^{j}}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}-\mathbb{I}\{{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{j}}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}){\mathbf{u}_{t}^{j}}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\right\|^{2}

Notice that:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{r=1}^{m}a_{j}(r)(\mathbb{I}\{{\mathbf{u}_{t+1}^{j}(r)}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}-\mathbb{I}\{{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{j}(r)}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{i}\})\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left|(\mathbb{I}\{{\mathbf{u}_{t+1}^{j}(r)}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}-\mathbb{I}\{{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{j}(r)}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{i}\})\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\sum_{r=1}^{m}\left|(\mathbb{I}\{{\mathbf{u}_{t+1}^{j}(r)}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{i}\}-\mathbb{I}\{{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{j}(r)}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{i}\})\right|\left\|{\mathbf{u}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(r)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}(|P(\bm{\theta}_{t+1},\bm{\alpha}_{i})|+|P(\bm{\theta}_{t},\bm{\alpha}_{i})|)\left\|\mathbf{u}_{t}^{\top}(r)-\mathbf{u}_{t+1}^{\top}(r)\right\|.

According to updating rule, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{u}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(r)-{\mathbf{u}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}(r)\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\eta\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}(r)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\eta\left\|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{u}_{t}^{j}}^{\top}(r)\bm{\alpha}_{i}\right\}\bm{\alpha}_{i}^{\top}a_{j}(r)(h^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-w^{t}_{i}(j))\right\|

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\left\|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{I}\left\{{\mathbf{u}^{j}_{t}(r)}^{\top}\bm{\alpha}_{i}\right\}\bm{\alpha}_{i}^{\top}a_{j}(r)(w^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(j)-w^{t}_{i}(j))\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})}}{\sqrt{m}}+\frac{2\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}

Since we prove that
\max_{r\in[m]}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{j}_{t+1}(r)-\mathbf{u}^{j}_{t}(r)\right\|\leq\rho
, we have:

\displaystyle|P(\bm{\theta}^{t+1},\bm{\alpha}_{i})|\leq m\rho+\sqrt{m\nu}.

Putting pieces together yields:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{m}\left({m}\rho+\sqrt{m\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{2\eta\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})}}{\sqrt{m}}+\frac{2\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{4}\left(\sqrt{m}\rho+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{4\eta^{2}{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})}}{{m}}+\frac{4\eta^{2}}{{m}}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}\right).

(28)

For
T_{2}
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\eta\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\frac{2}{n}\eta\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}(\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)))-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(\frac{2}{n}\eta\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}(\mathbf{w}^{t}(j)-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\inf_{q>0}\left(1+\frac{1}{q}\right)\frac{1}{n}\left\|\left(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2}{n}\eta{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}\right)(\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j))\right\|^{2}+(1+q)\eta^{2}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\frac{2}{n}{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}_{t}(\mathbf{w}^{t}(j)-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\inf_{q>0}\left(1+\frac{1}{q}\right)\left(1-\frac{2\eta\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t})}{n}\right)^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})+(1+q)\frac{4\eta^{2}}{n^{3}}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}(\mathbf{w}^{t}(j)-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j))\right\|^{2}

We choose
q=\frac{n}{2\eta\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t})}
, and the fact that
(1+a)(1-a)^{2}\leq(1-a)
:

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{2\eta\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t})}{n}\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})+\left(1+\frac{n}{2\eta\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t})}\right)\frac{4\eta^{2}}{n^{3}}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}(\mathbf{w}^{t}(j)-\mathbf{w}^{*}(j))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{2\eta\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t})}{n}\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1+\frac{n}{2\eta\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t})}\right)\frac{4\eta^{2}}{n^{3}}\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}

Since we choose
\eta\leq\frac{n}{2\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t})}
, we can re-write the above inequality as :

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{2\eta\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{t})}{n}\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})+\frac{4\eta}{n^{2}\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{t})}\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{2\eta\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0})}{n}+{4n\eta}\left(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})+\frac{4\eta\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}}{n^{2}\left(\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0})-2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)}

Plugging
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
back to (
27
), and using the eigenvalue lower bound from Lemma
10
(d) yields:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\inf_{p>0}\left(1+p\right)4\left(\sqrt{m}\rho+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{4\eta^{2}{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})}}{{m}}+\frac{4\eta^{2}}{{m}}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{p}\right)\Bigg[\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{n}+{4n\eta}\left(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{4\eta\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}}{n^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{0}-2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)}\Bigg].

Now we plug in
\rho=\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\inf_{p>0}\left(1+p\right)4\left(\sqrt{m}\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{4\eta^{2}{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})}}{{m}}+\frac{4\eta^{2}}{{m}}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1+\frac{1}{p}\right)\Bigg[\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{n}+{4n\eta}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{4\eta\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+2n^{2}(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}}{n^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{0}-2n^{2}(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)}\Bigg].

(29)

According to our choice of
m
:

\displaystyle m\geq\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\frac{256n^{4}}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}

we know that

\displaystyle{4n\eta}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\leq\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n},

\displaystyle 4\left(\sqrt{m}\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\frac{4\eta^{2}{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})}}{{m}}\leq\frac{\eta^{2}\lambda_{0}^{2}}{16n^{4}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t}),

\displaystyle 2n^{2}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\leq\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}.

Plugging above inequality back to Eq.(
29
) yields:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\inf_{p>0}\left(1+p\right)\left(4\left(\sqrt{m}\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{4\eta^{2}}{{m}}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}\right)+\frac{\eta^{2}\lambda_{0}^{2}}{16n^{4}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1+\frac{1}{p}\right)\left(1-\frac{3\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})+\left(1+\frac{1}{p}\right)\frac{4\eta\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}}{\frac{3}{8}\lambda_{0}n^{2}}.

According to the fact that:

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{1}{a}\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{2a-1}\right)=\frac{2a-2}{2a-1}\leq 1-\frac{1}{2a}

we choose
p=\frac{8n}{3\eta\lambda_{0}}-1
, and it yields:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8n}{3\eta\lambda_{0}}\left(4\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{4\eta^{2}}{{m}}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}\right)+\frac{\eta^{2}\lambda_{0}^{2}}{16n^{4}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\frac{3\eta\lambda_{0}}{8n}\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})+\left(1+\frac{1}{\frac{8n}{3\eta\lambda_{0}}-1}\right)\frac{4\eta\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}}{\frac{3}{8}\lambda_{0}n^{2}}.

Using the fact
\eta\leq\frac{n}{\lambda_{0}}
yields:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8n}{3\eta\lambda_{0}}\left(4\left(\frac{48dnB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{4\eta^{2}}{{m}}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\frac{3\eta\lambda_{0}}{8n}+\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{6n^{3}}\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})+\frac{8\eta\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}}{\frac{3}{8}\lambda_{0}n^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{128n\eta}{3\lambda_{0}{m}}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\frac{3\eta\lambda_{0}}{8n}+\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{6n^{3}}\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})+\frac{8\eta\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}_{0}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}}{\frac{3}{8}\lambda_{0}n^{2}}.

Since
n^{2}\geq\frac{4}{3}
, we know
\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{6n^{3}}\leq\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{8n}
. Hence we have:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{48dnB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{128n\eta}{3\lambda_{0}{m}}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})+\frac{8\eta\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}}{\frac{3}{8}\lambda_{0}n^{2}}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})+\frac{4n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\left(\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\frac{32\eta^{2}}{3{m}}+\frac{2\eta^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}}{\frac{3}{8}n^{2}}\right)(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D.

Plugging lower bound of
m\geq\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)^{2}\frac{256n^{4}}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}
yields:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})+\frac{4n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta^{2}}{24n^{4}}+\frac{2\eta^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}}{\frac{3}{8}n^{2}}\right)(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D.

Finally, according to inductive hypothesis (
I
), we plug in the convergence rate for
\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t})
and get

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t+1})
