\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})+\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)\sum_{s=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t-1-s}(1-\mu\gamma)^{s}C{\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}

\displaystyle+\frac{4n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta^{2}}{24n^{4}}+\frac{2\eta^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}}{\frac{3}{8}n^{2}}\right)(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})+C\sum_{s=0}^{t-1}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t-s}(1-\mu\gamma)^{s}{\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}

\displaystyle+\frac{4n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta^{2}}{24n^{4}}+\frac{2\eta^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}}{\frac{3}{8}n^{2}}\right)\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t-t}(1-\mu\gamma)^{t}(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D

Now we just need to ensure that

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta^{2}}{24n^{4}}+\frac{2\eta^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}}{\frac{3}{8}n^{2}}\leq C.

We hence choose
C
as:

\displaystyle C=\frac{\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta^{2}}{24n^{4}}+\frac{4\eta^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}}{\frac{3}{8}n^{2}}

Hence we proved that:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})+C\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t-s}(1-\mu\gamma)^{s}{\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})+\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}.

with

\displaystyle C=\frac{\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta^{2}}{24n^{4}}+\frac{4\eta^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}}{\frac{3}{8}n^{2}}.

∎

B.4
Relations Between Different Predictors

In this section, we are going to relate the predictions of neural network, random feature predictor and kernel OLS predictor.

Lemma 12

(Neural Network Predictor and Random Feature Predictor Coupling)
.

Let
\tilde{n}:=\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)
.
For Algorithm
2
, the following statement holds for any
j\in[d]
and
t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{h}_{t+1}^{j}-\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t+1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{16}{\lambda_{0}}\left(\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}+\left(2\sqrt{n}n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(2{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{n}+4\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}.

Proof.

Notice the following canonical decomposition:

\displaystyle h_{t+1}^{j}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t+1}(\bm{\alpha})

\displaystyle={\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}}^{\top}\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}

\displaystyle={\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}+({\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}}^{\top}\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}

\displaystyle={\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}({\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}-\eta\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t})+({\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}}^{\top}\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}

\displaystyle={\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}({\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}-\eta\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}({\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t})-\eta(\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}-\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}({\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t})))+({\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}}^{\top}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}-\eta\nabla_{\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}}\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{rf},j}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}))

\displaystyle=h_{t}^{j}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})+({\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}+\eta{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})-\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad+\eta({\bm{\phi}^{j}}^{\top}_{0}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\mathrm{rf},j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})-{\bm{\phi}^{j}}^{\top}_{t}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})).

Define
{r}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})={h}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-w_{*}(\bm{\alpha})(j)
, and
{r}_{t}^{\mathrm{rf}}(\bm{\alpha})={h}^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-w_{*}(\bm{\alpha})(j)
. Notice that

\displaystyle{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}}^{\top}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\mathrm{rf},j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})

\displaystyle={\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha})\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i}){r}^{\mathrm{rf}}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha})\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i}){r}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})

\displaystyle=-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha})\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})({h}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-{h}^{\mathrm{rf}}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i}))

\displaystyle\quad+\left({\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha})\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-{\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha})\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right){r}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})

We define the following stacked vector representation :

\displaystyle\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t+1}:=[{h^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{1}),...,{h^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{n})]^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{n},

\displaystyle\mathbf{r}_{t}:=[{r}_{t}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{1}),...,{r}_{t}^{\top}(\bm{\alpha}_{n})]^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}.

Hence we can write the pointwise difference into the following compact form:

\displaystyle\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t+1}-\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{0}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{0}\right)(\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t})

\displaystyle+\underbrace{({\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t+1}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}}_{T_{1}}

\displaystyle+\eta\underbrace{{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})-\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t})}_{T_{2}}

\displaystyle+\eta\underbrace{\frac{2}{n}\left({\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{0}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{0}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}\right)\mathbf{r}_{t}}_{T_{3}}.

Unrolling the recursion to
t=0
yields:

\displaystyle\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t+1}-\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t+1}

\displaystyle={\sum_{s=0}^{t}(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0})^{t-s}({\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s+1}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}}+{\eta\sum_{s=0}^{t}(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0})^{t-s}{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{s})-\mathbf{g}^{j}_{s})}

\displaystyle\quad+{\eta\sum_{s=0}^{t}(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0})^{t-s}\frac{2}{n}\left(\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{s}\right)\mathbf{r}_{s}}.

Taking spectral norm on both sides yields:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t+1}-\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t+1}\right\|

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\left\|\sum_{s=0}^{t}(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0})^{t-s}({\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s+1}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}\right\|}_{T_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\eta\left\|\sum_{s=0}^{t}(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0})^{t-s}{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{s})-\mathbf{g}^{j}_{s})\right\|}_{T_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\eta\left\|\sum_{s=0}^{t}(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0})^{t-s}\frac{2}{n}\left(\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{s}\right)\mathbf{r}_{s}\right\|}_{T_{3}}.

We first bound
T_{1}
as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0}\right)^{t-s}({\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s+1}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left\|(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0})^{t-s}\right\|\left\|({\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s+1}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left(1-\frac{2\eta}{n}\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0})\right)^{t-s}\left\|({\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s+1}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{n}\right)^{t-s}\left\|({\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s+1}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}\right\|

where we apply triangle inequality and Cauchy-Schwartz inequality. To bound
\left\|(\mathbf{\Phi}_{s+1}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Phi}_{s}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}_{t+1}\right\|
, we evoke Eq.(
28
)):

\displaystyle\left\|({\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s+1}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{n}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)\left(\frac{2\eta\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{s})}}{\sqrt{m}}+\frac{2\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Ks}D}\right)

Now we plug in the convergence rate from Theorem
5
, and obtain

\displaystyle\left\|({\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s+1}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}){\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{n}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)\left(\frac{2\eta\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{s}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})+\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}}{\sqrt{m}}+\frac{2\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Ks}D}\right)

Putting pieces together yields:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{n}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)\sum_{s=0}^{t}(1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{n})^{t-s}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\frac{2\eta\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{s}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})+\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}}{\sqrt{m}}+\frac{2\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Ks}D}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\eta\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{m}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)\sum_{s=0}^{t}(1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{n})^{t-s}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left({\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{s}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})+\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}}+\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Ks}D}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\eta\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{m}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)(1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{n})^{\frac{t}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{s=0}^{t}(1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{n})^{\frac{t}{2}-s}\left({\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{s}{2}}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})}+\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}}+(1-\mu\gamma)^{\frac{s}{2}}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}\right).

Using the fact that
1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{n}\leq 1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{4n}
and our choice of learning rate such that
1-\mu\gamma\leq 1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{4n}
, we have:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}4\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\left({(1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{n})^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}\eta}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})}+\frac{4n}{\lambda_{0}\eta}\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}}+(1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{n})^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}\eta}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}\right).

For
T_{2}
, similarly, we apply triangle and Cauchy-Schwartz inequality:

\displaystyle T_{2}\leq\eta\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{n}\right)^{t-s}\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{s})-\mathbf{g}^{j}_{s})\right\|

To bound
\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{s})-\mathbf{g}_{s})\right\|
, we evoke Lemma
11
:

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{s}}^{\top}(\nabla_{\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{s})-\mathbf{g}^{j}_{s})\right\|\leq\frac{2}{n}\left(\left\|\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0}\right\|+2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Ks}D}.

Putting the above inequality back to
T_{2}
yields:

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\eta\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{n}\right)^{t-s}\frac{2}{n}\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Ks}D}

\displaystyle\leq\eta\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\sum_{s=0}^{t}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}-\frac{s}{2}}\frac{2}{n}\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{16}{\lambda_{0}}\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}

For
T_{3}
, we also apply Cauchy-Schwartz and get:

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\eta\sum_{s=0}^{t}(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{n})^{t-s}\left\|\frac{2}{n}\left(\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{s}\right)\mathbf{r}_{s}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\eta\frac{2}{n}\sum_{s=0}^{t}(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{n})^{t-s}\left\|\left(\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{0}-\hat{\mathbf{K}}^{j}_{s}\right)\right\|\left\|\mathbf{r}_{s}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\eta\frac{2}{n}\sum_{s=0}^{t}(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{n})^{t-s}2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})}

where at last step we plug in empirical gram matrix bound from Lemma
10
. Now it suffices to plug in bound for
\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{t})

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\eta\frac{2}{n}\sum_{s=0}^{t}(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{n})^{t-s}2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{s}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})+\frac{4n\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}

\displaystyle\leq\eta\frac{2}{n}2n^{2}(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})\sqrt{n}\left((1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{n})^{\frac{t}{2}}\sum_{s=0}^{t}(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{n})^{\frac{t}{2}-\frac{s}{2}}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})}+\frac{8n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{4n\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta\sqrt{n}n\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\left((1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n})^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{8n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})}+\frac{8n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{4n\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}\right).

Putting pieces together yields:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t+1}-\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t+1}\right\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}4\eta}{\sqrt{m}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\left({(1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{4n})^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}\eta}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})}+\frac{4n}{\lambda_{0}\eta}\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}}+(1-\frac{\lambda_{0}\eta}{4n})^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}\eta}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{16}{\lambda_{0}}\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}

\displaystyle\quad+4\eta\sqrt{n}n\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)\left((1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n})^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{8n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})}+\frac{8n}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{4n\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}\right).

Let
\tilde{n}:=\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t+1}-\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t+1}\right\|\leq 4{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{n}\Bigg(\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{4n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}+\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{16}{\lambda_{0}}\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}

\displaystyle+4\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\left(\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})}+\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{16}{\lambda_{0}}\left(\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}+\left(2\sqrt{n}n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(2{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{n}+4\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}.

∎

Lemma 13

(Neural network parameter and RF parameter coupling)
.

For Algorithm
2
, the following statement holds for any
j\in[d]
and
t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\theta}_{t}^{j}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{n^{2}}{\lambda^{2}_{0}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)+\frac{n^{6}}{\lambda_{0}^{4}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\eta^{3}T^{2}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}\frac{n^{3}(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{3}}{(\lambda_{\max}+\lambda_{0})^{2}}+\frac{(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\right).

Proof.

According to updating rule of neural network and random feature predictor, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}-\eta(\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}-\nabla_{\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}}\mathcal{R}^{\mathrm{rf},j}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}))

\displaystyle=\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}-\eta\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\mathbf{r}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\mathbf{r}_{t}^{\mathrm{rf},j}(\bm{\alpha}_{i}))-\eta(\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}-\nabla_{{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}}\mathcal{R}^{j}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}))

\displaystyle=\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}-\eta\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\mathbf{r}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\bm{\phi}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\mathbf{r}_{t}^{\mathrm{rf},j}(\bm{\alpha}_{i}))-\eta(\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}-\nabla_{{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}}\mathcal{R}^{j}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}))

\displaystyle=\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}-\eta\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i}))\mathbf{r}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\eta\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(\mathbf{r}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{r}_{t}^{\mathrm{rf}}(\bm{\alpha}_{i}))-\eta(\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}-\nabla_{{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}}\mathcal{R}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}))

\displaystyle=\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}-\eta\frac{2}{n}(\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i}))\mathbf{r}_{t}-\eta\frac{2}{n}\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{h}_{t}^{\mathrm{rf},j})-\eta(\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}-\nabla_{{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}}\mathcal{R}^{j}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t})).

Now taking norm on both side yields:

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t+1}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t+1}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}\right\|+\eta\frac{2}{n}\underbrace{\left\|(\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{0})\mathbf{r}^{j}_{t}\right\|}_{T_{1}}+\eta\frac{2}{n}\underbrace{\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})(\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{h}_{t}^{\mathrm{rf},j})\right\|}_{T_{2}}+\eta\underbrace{\left\|\mathbf{g}^{j}_{t}-\nabla_{{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}}\mathcal{R}(\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t})\right\|}_{T_{3}}.

For
T_{1}
:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|(\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{0})\right\|\left\|\mathbf{r}^{j}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})}\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})}\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})+\frac{4n\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}.

where we plug in results from Lemma
10
and Theorem
5
.

For
T_{2}
:

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{\Phi}^{j}_{0}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{h}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{h}_{t}^{\mathrm{rf},j}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{16}{\lambda_{0}}\left(\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}+\left(2\sqrt{n}n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{n}\left(2{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{n}+4\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}.

For
T_{3}
, we evoke Lemma
11
:

\displaystyle T_{3}\leq 2\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{Kt}D}\leq 2\sqrt{(1-\frac{n\lambda_{0}}{4n})^{t}(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}.

Putting pieces together we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\theta}_{t+1}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{t+1}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t}\right\|+\frac{2\eta}{n}\sqrt{n(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})}\sqrt{n}\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{t}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})+\frac{4n\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta}{n}\sqrt{n}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{16}{\lambda_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left(\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}+\left(2\sqrt{n}n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta}{n}\sqrt{n}\left(2{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{n}+4\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta}{n}2\sqrt{(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n})^{t}(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}.

Conducting telescoping sum yields:

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\theta}_{t+1}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{t+1}\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{2\eta}{n}\sum_{s=0}^{t+1}n\sqrt{(\rho+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}})}\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{s}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{j}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{0})+\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta}{n}\sum_{s=0}^{t+1}\sqrt{n}\left(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n}\right)^{\frac{s}{2}}\frac{16}{\lambda_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\left(\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}+\left(2\sqrt{n}n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta}{n}\sum_{s=0}^{t+1}\left(2{{n}}\tilde{n}+4{n}^{2}\tilde{n}\right)\frac{8n}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2\eta}{n}\sum_{s=0}^{t+1}2\sqrt{(1-\frac{\eta\lambda_{0}}{4n})^{s}(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}

\displaystyle\leq n\sqrt{\tilde{n}}\left(\frac{16}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right)+\eta T\left(\sqrt{\tilde{n}}+\left(2n\tilde{n}+4n^{2}\tilde{n}\right)\frac{16}{\lambda_{0}}\right)\sqrt{\frac{4nC\left(1-\mu\gamma\right)^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{256\sqrt{n}}{\lambda^{2}_{0}}\left(\left(\frac{3}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+2n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}+\left(2\sqrt{n}n^{2}\tilde{n}+2\sqrt{n}n\tilde{n}\right)\sqrt{\widehat{\mathcal{R}}(\bm{\theta}_{0})}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{32}{\lambda_{0}}\sqrt{(1-\mu\gamma)^{K}D}

Recall that
\tilde{n}=\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)
and
C=\frac{\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta^{2}}{24n^{4}}+\frac{4\eta^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{K}\right\|+\frac{\lambda_{0}}{8}\right)^{2}}{\frac{3}{8}n^{2}}
, we conclude that:

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\theta}_{t+1}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}_{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{n^{2}}{\lambda^{2}_{0}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)+\eta^{2}T^{2}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}\frac{n^{4}}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\frac{n(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\eta\lambda_{0}}\frac{\eta^{2}(\lambda_{\max}+\lambda_{0})^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{n}{\lambda_{0}^{4}}n^{4}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D+\frac{n}{\lambda_{0}^{4}}n^{5}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}+\frac{(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{n^{2}}{\lambda^{2}_{0}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)+\eta^{3}T^{2}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}\frac{n^{3}(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{3}}{(\lambda_{\max}+\lambda_{0})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{n^{6}}{\lambda_{0}^{4}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}+\frac{(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\right)

∎

Theorem 6
.

For Algorithm
2
, the following statement holds true for any
j\in[d]
,
t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\alpha}\in\Delta^{N}}\left(h^{j}_{T}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{T}(\bm{\alpha})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}\right)^{2}(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu})^{2}+O\left(\frac{n^{2}}{\lambda^{2}_{0}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)+\frac{n^{6}}{\lambda_{0}^{4}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\eta^{3}T^{2}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}\frac{n^{3}(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{3}}{(\lambda_{\max}+\lambda_{0})^{2}}+\frac{(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\right)

If we choose
K\geq\kappa\log\left(\frac{\eta^{3}T^{2}n^{3}D}{\lambda_{0}^{3}}\right)
, then we have:

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{\alpha}\in\Delta^{N}}\left(h^{j}_{T}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{T}(\bm{\alpha})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\max\left\{\frac{\mathrm{poly}_{3}(n)}{\sqrt{m}},\frac{\mathrm{poly}_{8}(n)}{m}\right\}\right).

Proof.

We consider the following canonical decomposition:

\displaystyle\left(h^{j}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}(\bm{\alpha})\right)^{2}\leq 2\left(h^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})\right)^{2}+2\left(h^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})\right)^{2}

For the first term, we

\displaystyle\left\|h^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha})^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha})^{\top}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\rho^{2}(\sqrt{m}\rho+\sqrt{\nu})^{2}+O\left(\frac{n^{2}}{\lambda^{2}_{0}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)+\frac{n^{6}}{\lambda_{0}^{4}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\eta^{3}T^{2}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}\frac{n^{3}(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{3}}{(\lambda_{\max}+\lambda_{0})^{2}}+\frac{(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\right)

where we plug in Proposition
5
(e) to bound
\left\|\bm{\phi}^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha})^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}\right\|
, and use Lemma
13
to bound
\left\|\bm{\phi}^{j}_{0}(\bm{\alpha})^{\top}(\bm{\theta}^{j}_{t}-\bar{\bm{\theta}}^{j}_{t})\right\|
. Plugging in
\rho=\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}
will complete bounding first term:

\displaystyle\left\|h^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}\right)^{2}(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}}+\sqrt{\nu})^{2}+O\left(\frac{n^{2}}{\lambda^{2}_{0}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)+\frac{n^{6}}{\lambda_{0}^{4}}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\eta^{3}T^{2}\left(\frac{48nB_{y}}{\lambda_{0}\sqrt{m}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}\right)^{2}\frac{n^{3}(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{3}}{(\lambda_{\max}+\lambda_{0})^{2}}+\frac{(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\right)

For the second term, we use the intermediate result from
[
21
]

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{rf},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-\mathbf{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|\leq\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{m}}B_{y}\frac{n}{\lambda_{0}}\left(\frac{24n}{\lambda_{0}}+\frac{1}{2}\right)

∎

Lemma 14

(Approximation of Lipschitz functions on the ball
[
2
, Proposition 6]
)
.

For
R
larger than a constant
c
that depends only on
N
, for any function
f^{\star}:\mathbb{R}^{N}\to\mathbb{R}
such that for all
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{\tilde{x}}\in\mathbb{B}_{q}^{N}
,
\sup_{\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{B}_{q}^{N}}|f^{\star}(\mathbf{x})|\leq\Lambda
and
|f^{\star}(\mathbf{x})-f^{\star}(\mathbf{\tilde{x}})|\leq\Lambda\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{\tilde{x}}\|_{q}
, there exists
h\in\mathcal{H}
, such that
\|h\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\leq R
and

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{B}_{q}^{d}(1)}|f^{\star}(\mathbf{x})-h(\mathbf{x})|\leq A(R),\qquad A(R)=c\Lambda\left(\frac{\sqrt{R}}{\Lambda}\right)^{-\frac{2}{d-2}}\ln\left(\frac{\sqrt{R}}{\Lambda}\right)~.

Theorem 7

(
[
32
, Theorem 1]
)
.

Let
\mathcal{F}=\left\{f:\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\to[0,b]\right\}
for some
b<\infty
.
Then with probability at least
1-e^{-\nu},\nu>0
, for all
f\in\mathcal{F}
simultaneously

\displaystyle\|f\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|f\|_{n}^{2}+O\left(\|f\|_{n}\left(\widehat{\mathfrak{R}}(\mathcal{F})+\sqrt{\frac{b\nu}{n}}\right)+\widehat{\mathfrak{R}}^{2}(\mathcal{F})+\frac{b\nu}{n}\right)~,

where the worst-case empirical Rademacher complexity is defined as

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathfrak{R}}(\mathcal{F})=\sup_{\mathbf{x}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{x}_{n}\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}_{\bm{\varepsilon}}\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{i}f(\mathbf{x}_{i})\right|~.

(
\varepsilon_{1},\ldots,\varepsilon_{n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathrm{iid}}}{{\sim}}\mathrm{unif}\{\pm 1\}
)

Lemma 15
.

[Rademacher complexity of kernel predictor class
[
3
]
Lemma 22]
Consider the following function class with bounded RKHS norm:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}=\left\{h(\mathbf{x})=\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}k(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{x}_{i}):h\in\mathcal{H},\left\|h\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}\leq B\right\}.

Then its empirical Rademacher complexity is bounded by:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathfrak{R}}(\mathcal{F})\leq O\left(\frac{B}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

Lemma 16

(Excess risk of virtual KLS predictor learnt on optimal KLS approximator)
.

Let
h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}
be the optimal approximator of
f^{*,j}
in RKHS, and
\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})
be KLS predictor trained by GD on sample
\{\bm{\alpha}_{i},h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n}
. Then the following excess risk bound holds with probability at least
1-e^{-\nu}
:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t+1}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{2\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta}{n}\right)^{t}+O\left(\left(1-\frac{2\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta}{n}\right)^{t}\left(\frac{R}{\sqrt{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{n}}\right)+\frac{R^{2}}{n}+\frac{\nu}{n}\right)~,

Proof.

Given a fixed function
h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}
with
\left\|h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}\right\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}\leq R
, we consider the following class:

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}=\left\{\tilde{h}(\mathbf{x})=h(\mathbf{x})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}(\mathbf{x}):h\in\mathcal{H},\left\|h\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}\leq B\right\}

According to constant shift property of Rademacher complexity
[
33
]
and Lemma
15
, we know

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathfrak{R}}(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}})\leq O\left(\frac{B}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{R}}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

According to Theorem
7
, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}\left(\frac{B+\sqrt{R}}{\sqrt{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\nu}{n}}\right)+\frac{(B+\sqrt{R})^{2}}{n}+\frac{\nu}{n}\right)~,

Now, it suffices to bound (i) empirical risk term
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}
and (ii) RKHS norm of
\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}
.

For (i), we define
\widehat{\mathcal{R}}_{t}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{i})\right\|^{2}
and recall the updating rule of kernel OLS predictor:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t+1}-\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}=\mathbf{K}\mathbf{c}^{j}_{t+1}-\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{K}\mathbf{c}^{j}_{t}-\mathbf{K}\frac{2\eta}{n}\mathbf{K}^{\top}(\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}-\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*})-\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\mathbf{K}^{\top}\mathbf{K}\right)(\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}-\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*})

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\mathbf{K}^{\top}\mathbf{K}\right)^{t}(\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{0}-\tilde{\mathbf{h}}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}).

Hence we know that:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{R}}_{t+1}=\left(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\mathbf{K}\mathbf{K}^{\top}\right)^{t}\widehat{\mathcal{R}}_{0}.

(ii) RKHS norm of
\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\mathbf{I}-\left(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\mathbf{K}\right)^{t}\right)\mathbf{y}_{*}\right\|_{\mathbf{K}^{-1}}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left(\mathbf{I}-\left(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\mathbf{K}\right)^{t}\right)\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{K}\mathbf{c}_{*}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{I}-\left(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\mathbf{K}\right)^{t}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{K}^{-1}\mathbf{K}\mathbf{c}_{*}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbf{I}-\left(\mathbf{I}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\mathbf{K}\right)^{t}\right\|\left\|\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}\leq\sqrt{R}.

∎

Lemma 17
.

[
21
, Lemma 8]

Let
f^{\mathrm{\kappa}}_{t},\tilde{f}^{\mathrm{\kappa}}_{t}
be
GD
-trained
KLS
predictors (as discussed in
section

B.2
) given training samples
(\mathbf{x}_{i},y_{i})_{i=1}^{n}
and
(\mathbf{x}_{i},\tilde{y}_{i})_{i=1}^{n}
respectively, where
y_{i}=f^{\star}(\mathbf{x}_{i})+\varepsilon_{i}
and
\tilde{y}_{i}=h(\mathbf{x}_{i})+\varepsilon_{i}
with
\|h\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\leq R
characterized by
lemma

14
.
Then, with
A(R)
defined in
lemma

14
, for any
t\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\|f^{\mathrm{\kappa}}_{t}-\tilde{f}^{\mathrm{\kappa}}_{t}\|_{n}^{2}\leq A(R)~.

B.5
Proof of Theorem
3

Proof.

We notice the following risk decomposition:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|h^{j}_{T}(\bm{\alpha})-w^{*}_{j}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|h^{j}_{T}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{T}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}+4\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{T}(\bm{\alpha})-\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{T}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+4\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\tilde{h}^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}+4\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|h^{\mathrm{kls},j}_{*}(\bm{\alpha})-w^{*}_{j}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\max\left\{\frac{\mathrm{poly}_{3}(n)}{\sqrt{m}},\frac{\mathrm{poly}_{8}(n)}{{m}}\right\}\right)+O\left(\left(1+\frac{c}{\lambda_{0}^{2}}\right)A(R)^{2}+\frac{c}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\frac{2\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta}{n}\right)^{T}+O\left(\frac{R}{n}\right)+O\left(\Lambda\left(\frac{\sqrt{R}}{\Lambda}\right)^{-\frac{2}{N-2}}\ln\left(\frac{\sqrt{R}}{\Lambda}\right)\right),

where respectively we plug in the NN-KLS coupling result (Theorem
6
), Lemma
17
, excess risk of KLS (Lemma
16
) and approximation error of RKHS to Lipschitz function (Lemma
14
).
By choosing
m\geq\Omega(n^{8+\frac{2}{2+N}})
,
T\geq\Omega\left(\frac{n}{N\lambda_{0}^{2}\eta}\log(n)\right)
and optimizing over
R
, we recover the rate:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|h^{j}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-w^{*}_{j}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}\leq O\left(\Lambda^{2}n^{-\frac{2}{2+N}}\right).

Hence by summing over all coordinates
j\in[d]
we conclude the result:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{h}_{t}(\bm{\alpha})-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha})\right\|^{2}\leq O\left(\Lambda^{2}dn^{-\frac{2}{2+N}}\right)

where in our case
lemma

1
implies
\Lambda=\kappa^{*}
.
Finally we lower bound
\lambda_{0}
using
[
29
, Theorem 3.2]
which implies that

\lambda_{0}=\Omega(\mathrm{polylog}(n,N)N)
with probability at least
1-n^{2}e^{-O(\sqrt{N})}
for a distribution on a simplex.
∎

Appendix C
Regret Analysis of Algorithm
4

In this section, we are going to provide the proof for Theorem
4
. Let us first introduce the following definitions that will be used in our proof.

Definition 2

(Covering and Packing Numbers)
.

An
\varepsilon
-cover of a set
S
w.r.t. some metric
\|\cdot\|
is a set
\left\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}_{n}\right\}\subseteq S

such that for each
\mathbf{x}\in S
there exists
i\in\{1,\ldots,n\}
such that
\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}_{i}\|\leq\varepsilon
. The covering number
\mathcal{N}(S,\varepsilon,\|\cdot\|)
is the smallest cardinality of a
\varepsilon
-cover.
An
\varepsilon
-packing of a set
S
w.r.t. some metric
\|\cdot\|
is a set
\left\{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{x}^{\prime}_{m}\right\}\subseteq S

such that for any distinct
i,j\in\{1,\ldots,m\}
, we have
\|\mathbf{x}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}_{j}\|>\varepsilon
.
The packing number
\mathcal{M}(S,\varepsilon,\|\cdot\|)
is the largest cardinality of a
\varepsilon
-packing.

It is well known that

\mathcal{M}(S,2\varepsilon,\|\cdot\|)\leq\mathcal{N}(S,\varepsilon,\|\cdot\|)\leq\mathcal{M}(S,\varepsilon,\|\cdot\|)
.

Definition 3

(Metric dimension)
.

The metric space
(\mathcal{X},\|\cdot\|)
had metric dimension
d
, if there exists a constant
C_{d}
such that for all
\varepsilon>0
,
\mathcal{X}
has an
\varepsilon
-cover at most
C_{d}\varepsilon^{-d}
.

Proof.

We start from the standard analysis framework for online non-parametric regression
[
13
,
18
,
20
]
.
Let
S_{t}
be the value of the variable
S
at the end of time
t
. Hence
S_{0}=\varnothing
. The functions
\pi_{t}:\mathcal{X}\to\{1,\dots,t\}
for
t=1,2,\dots
map each data point
\mathbf{x}
to its closest center (in norm
\left\|\,\cdot\,\right\|
) in
S_{t-1}
,

\pi_{t}(\bm{\alpha})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{cl}{\argmin_{s\in S_{t-1}}\left\|\bm{\alpha}-\bm{\alpha}_{s}\right\|}&\text{if $S_{t-1}\not=\varnothing$}\\
t&\text{otherwise.}\end{array}\right.

The set
T_{s}
contain all data points
\bm{\alpha}_{t}
that at time
t
belonged to the ball with center
\bm{\alpha}_{s}
and radius
\varepsilon_{t}
,

T_{s}=\left\{{t}\,:\,{\left\|\bm{\alpha}_{t}-\bm{\alpha}_{s}\right\|\leq\varepsilon_{t},\,t=s,\dots,T}\right\}~.

Finally,
\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{s}
is the best fixed prediction for all examples
(\bm{\alpha}_{t},\mathbf{w}_{t})
such that
t\in T_{s}
,

\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{s}=\argmin_{\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{W}}\sum_{t\in T_{s}}\ell_{t}(\mathbf{w})=\frac{1}{|T_{s}|}\sum_{t\in T_{s}}\mathbf{w}_{t}~.

(30)

We proceed by decomposing the regret into estimation and approximation cumulative errors,

R_{T}(f)=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}\big(f(\bm{\alpha}_{t})\big)\Big)=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}\big(\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{{\pi_{t}{(\bm{\alpha}_{t})}}}\big)\Big)+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\ell_{t}\big(\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{{\pi_{t}{(\bm{\alpha}_{t})}}}\big)-\ell_{t}\big(f(\bm{\alpha}_{t})\big)\Big)~.

The estimation term is bounded as

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}\big(\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{{\pi_{t}{(\bm{\alpha}_{t})}}}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle=\sum_{s\in S_{T}}\sum_{t\in T_{s}}\Big(\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}(\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{s})\Big)

We study the cumulative regret within a fixed ball
T_{s}
. We use
T_{s}(t)
to denote the set of all points belong to ball with center
\mathbf{w}_{s}
, at time
t
. That is,

\displaystyle T_{s}(t)=\{j:j\in T_{s},j\leq t\}.

One property of
T_{s}(t)
is that, if
\mathbf{x}_{t}\in T_{s}
, then
|T_{s}(t)|=|T_{s}(t-1)|+1
.
We further define
\tilde{\mathbf{w}}_{t}=\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t)|}\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t)}\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})
.
According to
[
6
, Lemma 3.1]
, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in T_{s}}\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}(\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{s})\leq\sum_{t\in T_{s}}\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}(\tilde{\mathbf{w}}_{t})

Recall that
\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t}=\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t-1)|}\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t-1)}\mathbf{w}_{j}
. Then,

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}[\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}(\tilde{\mathbf{w}}_{t})]

\displaystyle\leq 4D\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t}-\ell_{t}(\tilde{\mathbf{w}}_{t})\right\|

\displaystyle=4D\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t-1)|}\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t-1)}\mathbb{I}\left\{Z_{j}=1\right\}\mathbf{w}_{j}-\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t)|}\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t)}\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 4D\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t-1)|}\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t-1)}\mathbb{I}\left\{Z_{j}=1\right\}(\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{j}))\right\|

\displaystyle\quad+4D\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t-1)|}\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t-1)}\mathbb{I}\left\{Z_{j}=1\right\}\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})-\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t)|}\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t)}\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})\right\|

According to the convergence rate of GD on strongly-convex and smooth function, the first term is bounded by
p(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D
. For second term:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t-1)|}\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t-1)}\mathbb{I}\left\{Z_{j}=1\right\}\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})-\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t)|}\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t)}\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t-1)}\left(\frac{\mathbb{I}\left\{Z_{j}=1\right\}}{|T_{s}(t-1)|}-\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t)|}\right)\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t-1)}\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}_{Z_{j}}\left\|\left(\frac{\mathbb{I}\left\{Z_{j}=1\right\}}{|T_{s}(t-1)|}-\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t)|}\right)\mathbf{w}^{*}(\bm{\alpha}_{j})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in T_{s}(t-1)}\left(p\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t-1)||T_{s}(t)|}+(1-p)\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t)|}\right)D

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(p\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t)|}+(1-p)\right)D

where at the second last step we use the fact that
|T_{s}(t)|=|T_{s}(t-1)|+1
.

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in T_{s}}\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}(\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{s})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t\in T_{s}}\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}(\tilde{\mathbf{w}}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq 4D\sum_{t\in T_{s}}\left(p\frac{1}{|T_{s}(t)|}+(1-p)\right)D+4pD|T_{s}|(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D

We further sum above statement over all
s\in S_{T}
:

\displaystyle\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{s\in S_{T}}\sum_{t\in T_{s}}\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}(\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{s})\right]

\displaystyle\leq p\sum_{s\in S_{T}}\left(\ln(|T_{s}|)+(1-p)TD+4pD|T_{s}|(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D\right)

\displaystyle\leq p|S_{T}|\ln(T)+(1-p)TD+4pDT(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D

The first inequality is a known bound on the regret under square loss
[
6
, page 43]
. We upper bound the size of the final packing
S_{T}
as

|S_{T}|\leq\mathcal{M}\big(B,\varepsilon_{T},\left\|\,\cdot\,\right\|\big)\leq C_{N}\,\varepsilon_{T}^{-N}

Thus,

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\ell_{t}\big(\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{{\pi_{t}{(\bm{\alpha}_{t})}}}\big)\Big)\leq pC_{N}\varepsilon_{T}^{-N}\ln(T)+(1-p)TD+4pDT(1-\frac{1}{\kappa})^{K}D

(31)

Next, we bound the approximation term. Using (
30
) we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\ell_{t}\big(\mathbf{w}^{\star}_{{\pi_{t}{(\bm{\alpha}_{t})}}}\big)-\ell_{t}\big(f(\bm{\alpha}_{t})\big)\Big)\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\ell_{t}\big(f(\bm{\alpha}_{{\pi_{t}{(\bm{\alpha}_{t})}}})\big)-\ell_{t}\big(f(\bm{\alpha}_{t})\big)\Big)~.

(32)

Note that
\ell_{t}
is
4D
-Lipschitz because
\mathbf{y}_{t},\hat{\mathbf{y}}_{t}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}(D)
. Hence,

\displaystyle\ell_{t}\big(f(\bm{\alpha}_{{\pi_{t}{(\bm{\alpha}_{t})}}})\big)-\ell_{t}\big(f(\bm{\alpha}_{t})\big)\leq 4D\big|f(\bm{\alpha}_{{\pi_{t}{(\bm{\alpha}_{t})}}})-f(\bm{\alpha}_{t})\big|\leq 4D\kappa^{*}\|\bm{\alpha}_{{\pi_{t}{(\bm{\alpha}_{t})}}}-\bm{\alpha}_{t}\|\leq 4D\varepsilon_{t}

by
\kappa^{*}
-Lipschitzness of
f
.
Thus, putting all together,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ell_{t}(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t})-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ell_{t}(f(\bm{\alpha}_{t}))\leq 8\ln(eT)C_{N}\,\varepsilon_{T}^{-N}+4D\kappa^{*}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\varepsilon_{t}

and
recalling that
\varepsilon_{t}=t^{-\frac{1}{1+N}}
we have

\sum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\frac{1}{1+N}}\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\frac{1}{1+N}}\leq\int_{0}^{T}\tau^{-\frac{1}{1+N}}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau=\left(1+\frac{1}{N}\right)T^{\frac{N}{1+N}}\leq 2T^{\frac{N}{1+N}}

which completes the proof.

∎
